ODIS regional and town buses · Novojičínsko Východ · Novojičínsko Západ · Bílovecko · MHD Nový Jičín · Studénka · vypravenost · Tuesday 20 Oct 2026

ODIS Novojičínsko Bílovecko

The ODIS bus lines of three regional contract areas (Novojičínsko Východ, Z-Group bus; Novojičínsko Západ and Bílovecko, Transdev Morava) and the town lines of Nový Jičín (ARRIVA autobusy) and Studénka (Transdev Morava), set as Simutrans lines with the owner's regional method: each line read over the whole day at its core points, a repeating interval, destination and shift variants on their own longer intervals (at most 480 min), timetables at the terminals worked back from the knots, every afternoon stop on a line, buses sized from the pattern. Buses are the ones seen on each line on the ODIS live map on Tuesday 20 Oct 2026.

Network state, 20 Oct 2026

What is in the model and what is left out, with the reason.

modelledThree regional contract areas and two town networks

Novojičínsko Východ (licences 883xxx, Z-Group bus, Kopřivnice), Novojičínsko Západ (882xxx) and Bílovecko (884xxx) of Transdev Morava; the town lines of Nový Jičín (886xxx, ARRIVA autobusy) and Studénka (887611, Transdev Morava). Lines holding licences in two areas are shown under the area with most of their trips.

modelledDiversion timetables to the end of the timetable year

The CIS JŘ export has diversion versions valid on the vehicle day until 11–12 Dec 2026 for 286, 623, 641 (via Příbor, Štramberská), 662, 673 (Březová, škola instead of Březová, střed), 679, 692 and 693. They run until the timetable change, so they are modelled as they run.

left outShort diversions: 629, 643, 929

629 (to 3 Dec) and 929 (to 12 Nov) only move times; 643 (16 – 31 Oct) runs via Příbor, Štramberská. They end before the timetable change, so the regular timetables are modelled (shared rule: short works as the regular state).

left outLines on other sheets

351, 355, 980 and 985 hold a few trips on Novojičínsko licences but are Frýdecko-Místecko lines, on the Těšínsko/Třinecko/Frýdecko sheet. 261, 263, 270, 285 and 678 are modelled on the Opavsko/Vítkovsko/Hlučínsko sheet, where most of their trips are. 655 is modelled here: 41 of its 45 trips are on the Novojičínsko licence.

Each bus shows its fleet number and branch, full type and length (with its number plate), and pak128 core with livery. Green border: seen running this line on the live map that afternoon (the line's window first; the trip is named). Dashed border: designed, not seen in the window, with the reason. Dimmed, dotted: seen, not needed on this line.

How to read the line cards

Typing the timetable. Values go into the Scheduling tab of the terminal entry, with the calendar on: Departure every, Offset, Also at (at most 7). Offsets count from 01:00 and every terminal is set as a loop, so each value is (real departure − 61) mod interval; the real clock minute is under every value. A row with Every 0 has no timetable: the bus leaves on arrival.

Core points and knots. Each line was read over the whole operating day (5:00–23:00) at its core points: terminals, knots where variants split, shared hubs. A trip counts at the time it passes a core point, wherever it starts. Where a slot is defined at a knot, the terminal departure is the knot time minus the running time, and the knot gets no timetable.

Variants. Each destination, path or shift variant is its own Simutrans line on a longer interval (the save game's Ex4). Shift lines repeat every 480 (8 h), the longest interval allowed. Trips more than 3 min off every slot of the line are off the pattern and shown in orange.

Buses needed. One bus followed round the line's own slots with the real running times and at least 3 min at each terminal (less where the slots chain closer, as the real timetable turns). Real buses swap lines at Nový Jičín, Kopřivnice, Frenštát, Bílovec and Ostrava-Svinov all afternoon, so per-line totals are higher than what Transdev runs; pooling lines would be the owner's choice and is not done.

To check

First, four choices made here that you may want to flip, each with its alternative. Then the Simutrans lines whose model window has a slot without a real departure, or a real departure more than 3 min off: mostly the real timetable is irregular there, and the card says why.

Novojičínsko Východ

Licences 883xxx, Z-Group bus provozovna Kopřivnice (fleet numbers 63-). 28 lines on this sheet as 31 Simutrans lines, 51 buses: 51 seen on the ODIS live map, 0 designed. At 16:00 Z-Group had 33 buses on the road on these lines: the real buses swap lines at the terminals, while each Simutrans line here has its own (pooling would be the owner’s choice). Kopřivnice, Příbor, Frenštát pod Radhoštěm and the Ostrava – Brušperk – Hukvaldy corridor. The Kopřivnice town loop (station – Kolonie – Družební) runs inside licence 883653.

Pak128 objects

VZ-ODIS-bus holds the ODIS regional fleet; Transdev Morava's white with the red Transdev logo is transdev, Z-Group's white with the kraj logo bila, BORS Břeclav's yellow-blue borszlutomodra. The Nový Jičín and Studénka town buses take their town packages. Objects confirmed by the pak fleet desk on 21 Oct 2026; the Z-Group-livery Crossway LE Line (21 Oct) and ARRIVA's red-black SOR BN 10,5 no. 7577 (22 Oct; its layout is unverified, as no photo after the 2025 repaint exists) were drawn for this sheet. Objects are read from the installed pak (VZ-ODIS-bus, VZ-MHDNovyJicin-bus, VZ-MHDStudenka-bus); “≈” marks a stand-in, listed in pak_request.md. Length is in car units (16 = one tile).

How this was built

Scope. Licences with the area prefixes 883 (Novojičínsko Východ, Z-Group bus), 882 (Novojičínsko Západ) and 884 (Bílovecko) of Transdev Morava, 886 (MHD Nový Jičín, ARRIVA autobusy) and 887 (Studénka, Transdev Morava) in the CIS JŘ bus export (CIS JŘ bus JDF export of 20 Oct 2026 (stored by the Havířov/Karviná/Orlová pipeline)), evaluated for Tuesday 20 Oct 2026 with the shared version rule. The prefixes match the live map (Z-Group pr. Kopřivnice with 63- buses; Transdev Morava pr. Nový Jičín 62- and pr. Ostrava 64-; ARRIVA Nový Jičín) and the kraj's contract areas (Novojičínsko Východ, Novojičínsko Západ, Bílovecko). 90 ODIS lines; 63 are modelled here, 9 are on other sheets, the rest are listed with the reason.

Method. pipeline/bus-schedule-method.md (v3, the owner's regional rules of 20 Oct 2026): whole-day reading at core points, repeating interval, Ex4 variants, timing at terminals worked back from knots, no daily lines (at most every 480), every afternoon stop on a line, buses from the pattern, every line checked by hand. Each line's choices are in config.json → lines and in the README.

Buses. The ODIS live map (mpvnet.cz) polled every minute 13:05–19:30 on Tuesday 20 Oct 2026 by the Havířov/Karviná/Orlová pipeline gives every bus's trips (licence and trip number), joined to fleet numbers by number plate (seznam-autobusu.cz register). A bus fills one Simutrans line it ran in that line's window, by matching. Buses already on another sheet (Ostrava Bus Vypravenost, section All lines at Hranečník; Havířov Karviná Orlová Bus Vypravenost (town lines); ODIS Regional Havířovsko Karvinsko Orlovsko; ODIS Těšínsko Třinecko Frýdecko; ODIS Opavsko Vítkovsko Hlučínsko) are not used here. Fleet numbers and types come from the seznam-autobusu registers of Z-Group bus and Transdev Morava; ARRIVA's town buses and a few unlisted plates were looked up one by one (01b_plates.py).

Sources: CIS JŘ bus JDF, ODIS live map, Transdev Slezsko register, Z-Group bus register, Transdev Morava register.
